# Premier League Player Attributes — Starting Database Audit

**Game version:** 2026.10.25.1
**Season:** 2026  
**Scope:** All 1,343 player records at the 20 Premier League clubs in a fresh career: 543 calibrated first-team (A), 400 reserve (R), and 400 youth (Y) players. First-team CA/PA and attributes use the supplied calibrated rosters and the documented derivation; reserve/youth records are unchanged.

**Attribute scale:** 1–20. First-team **CA** uses the position weights and conversion in `docs/calibrated-pl-rosters/Part-2-Rating-Provenance-and-CA-PA-Derivation.md`. **PA** is derived by applying the age-banded rule to each supplied PA–CA gap. **Inspired by** is the biography reference, not the player’s display name.

## Reading the ratings

- Attributes are position-specific building blocks; CA is calculated from the player’s main position and is not a universal league-wide grade.
- Compare players by position and attribute profile as well as CA. A high CA does not mean every attribute is high.
- The first-team summary is the quickest way to spot club-level rating inflation. The full profiles below let you see which skills drive those ratings.
